## `AFG-9820-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Drinking Water Inspector
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Self-Made Furniture Joint Strength & Well Water Total Dissolved Solids
- **Intro:** Drinking Water Inspector — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Self-Made Furniture Joint Strength & Well Water Total Dissolved Solids — Verify Steady-State Environmental Conditions: Temperature: 20±2°C; Humidity: 50±10% RH; Vibration: <0.5 mm/s RMS
- **Key tolerance:** Temperature: 20±2°C; Humidity: 50±10% RH; Vibration: <0.5 mm/s RMS
- **Standards:** `ISO 10523:2008`, `ISO 7888:1985`, `ISO 5725-1:1994`, `Afghanistan National Standards Authority (ANSA)`
